# Skills · 0-9 · part 18

689 entries, {{SKILL_PREFIX}}-fix-branch to ローカル環境操作指示. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **{{SKILL_PREFIX}}-fix-branch**: Cut one branch for fixing one or several GitHub issues, asking once which branch they belong to by listing the open feature/* branches with main as the last option. A fix for a feature in flight branches from that feature and is tested when the feature merges; a production fix with no owning feature branches from main and must pass pest before it merges, so batching several production fixes costs one pest run instead of one each. Records the target on every issue. Never writes the fix itself. ([AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template](https://github.com/AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/%7B%7BSKILL_PREFIX%7D%7D-fix-branch))
- **{{SKILL_PREFIX}}-merge**: Run the merge for one pull request, a task PR into its feature/* branch or a feature branch into main, with the gates each kind needs: check the gates with definite questions rather than inference, squash-merge on the operator's confirmation, reconcile the local machine, regenerate the documentation indexes from post-merge main, and carry them in a documentation-only follow-up PR. Never merges over a red or pending check, never launches /code-review, never bounces the dev stack. ([AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template](https://github.com/AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/%7B%7BSKILL_PREFIX%7D%7D-merge))
- **{{SKILL_PREFIX}}-po-check**: Ask the product owner node whether promoting one backlog item now is consistent with the roadmap, the live batches and the constitution, without promoting it. Returns promote, challenge or defer with the grounds. Read-only; changes nothing. The {{SKILL_PREFIX}}-promote-backlog-spec skill runs the same check before it acts. ([AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template](https://github.com/AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/%7B%7BSKILL_PREFIX%7D%7D-po-check))
- **{{SKILL_PREFIX}}-prepare-docs-reference**: Regenerate docs/reference.md, the annotated index of every document the claude.ai project syncs from this repository, then rebuild docs/claude-transfer/ (the small subset uploaded as project files for mobile) and its own reference.md from the membership file, reporting anything that would make either set misleading. Run from post-merge main, as the fourth phase of the {{SKILL_PREFIX}}-merge skill or by hand on main, never on a branch that has yet to merge. ([AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template](https://github.com/AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/%7B%7BSKILL_PREFIX%7D%7D-prepare-docs-reference))
- **{{SKILL_PREFIX}}-promote-backlog-spec**: Promote one backlog item into the live spec view, allocating a spec number confirmed against the repository, renaming ft- to spec-NNN-, restating the document's identity block, cutting and pushing the spec's feature/NNN-slug branch from main, and rewriting every reference. One item per invocation; leaves the document change staged for the operator to review and commit. ([AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template](https://github.com/AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/%7B%7BSKILL_PREFIX%7D%7D-promote-backlog-spec))
- **{{SKILL_PREFIX}}-promote-wp**: Promote one work package from docs/work-packages/backlog/ into the live folder because work on it is about to begin, cutting and pushing its feature/wp-slug branch from main and writing the Target branch line. Optionally gives it a batch number the operator names. One package per invocation; leaves the document change staged for the operator to review and commit. ([AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template](https://github.com/AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/%7B%7BSKILL_PREFIX%7D%7D-promote-wp))
- **{{SKILL_PREFIX}}-review-backlog**: Run the backlog review ceremony. Walks docs/features/backlog/ against a named phase one item at a time, asks where each belongs, promotes the accepted ones into numbered specs, and writes a dated record. Full mode for phase planning, blockers-only for a quick post-merge sweep. Surfaces decisions rather than making them. ([AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template](https://github.com/AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/%7B%7BSKILL_PREFIX%7D%7D-review-backlog))
- **{{SKILL_PREFIX}}-review-session-notes**: Process session briefs from docs/sessions/ into the artefacts they declare (backlog features, work packages, documentation edits and GitHub issues), then mark each brief processed. Named brief, or every unprocessed brief when given no argument. Leaves file changes staged for the operator to review and commit, and raises issues directly. ([AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template](https://github.com/AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/%7B%7BSKILL_PREFIX%7D%7D-review-session-notes))
- **{{SKILL_PREFIX}}-session-name**: Give the operator the exact /rename command that names the current Claude Code session for the work it holds, so a session list across two machines shows which spec or merge each session belongs to. Spec mode (the default, run as an after_specify hook) derives the heading from the active spec; done mode (called by the {{SKILL_PREFIX}}-merge skill as its last step) prefixes DONE. Never claims a rename it did not perform, because it cannot perform one. ([AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template](https://github.com/AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/%7B%7BSKILL_PREFIX%7D%7D-session-name))
- **{{SKILL_PREFIX}}-split-feature**: Right-size one backlog feature capture before it is promoted. Lists the capabilities in it, tests each for whether it can ship on its own, and proposes either an ordered split into independently shippable slices or keeping it whole, with reasons. Writes nothing until the operator approves; on approval writes each slice as its own linked ft- capture and restates the original as their parent. Never runs spec-kit. ([AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template](https://github.com/AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/%7B%7BSKILL_PREFIX%7D%7D-split-feature))
- **{{SKILL_PREFIX}}-switch-feature**: Point spec-kit at a different existing spec by setting feature_directory in .specify/feature.json, after verifying the spec exists and is in flight. Reports the active path and what state that spec is in. With no argument, reports the current feature and lists what is available. Never creates specs, runs phases, or changes spec-kit itself. ([AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template](https://github.com/AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/%7B%7BSKILL_PREFIX%7D%7D-switch-feature))
- **{{SKILL_PREFIX}}-sync-local**: Bring this machine up to date after a merge, here or on the other machine, onto the branch the merge landed in (main, or a feature/* branch for a task PR). Refuses to pull into unmerged work; switches off a merged branch and deletes it once the squash is verified on its target, pulls the target fast-forward only, runs plain migrate only on the operator's yes in the moment, says whether a dev stack bounce is needed and gives the command, and runs the duplicate work package check. Phase 3 of {{SKILL_PREFIX}}-merge, and runnable on its own. Never bounces the stack, never runs a destructive datab… ([AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template](https://github.com/AnalysisByDesign/laravel_template/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/%7B%7BSKILL_PREFIX%7D%7D-sync-local))

- **{{skills.prefix}}-fix-process**: Start: a diagnostic is needed, or a warning, error, exception path, broken connection, or measured bottleneck appears. Action: apply the C06 diagnostic, FIX, and exception-handling processes with the project's real inputs, proving what is missing, why, and how the fix fills it. End: the omission is filled at its owner and never hidden. ([GarnetRapture/SimpleEngineering](https://github.com/GarnetRapture/SimpleEngineering/tree/HEAD/src/template/skills/fix-process) · ★2)
- **{{skills.prefix}}-inline**: Start: a file of the primary language ({{generated.primaryLanguage}}) is edited. Action: keep comments, types, styles, handlers, and magic literals out of the edited code by moving each into its domain owner, following the enabled C04 inline rules and their good and bad examples. End: the edit adds no inline construct that an enabled rule bans, and the project hook accepts it. ([GarnetRapture/SimpleEngineering](https://github.com/GarnetRapture/SimpleEngineering/tree/HEAD/src/template/skills/inline) · ★2)
- **{{skills.prefix}}-mcp**: Start: tool discovery, combined information, or convention and process guidance is needed from the project's registered MCP servers. Action: discover registered schemas, batch only eligible reads, and call actions individually. End: exact selected information and every result are consumed without granting action authority. ([GarnetRapture/SimpleEngineering](https://github.com/GarnetRapture/SimpleEngineering/tree/HEAD/src/template/skills/mcp) · ★2)
- **{{skills.prefix}}-naming**: Start: a file of the primary language ({{generated.primaryLanguage}}) is created, renamed, or edited, or a symbol is named. Action: place it by the C04 domain structure and name it by the C05 naming rules, checking the good and bad examples. End: every new or renamed path and symbol states its domain, responsibility, and result, and every consumer is connected. ([GarnetRapture/SimpleEngineering](https://github.com/GarnetRapture/SimpleEngineering/tree/HEAD/src/template/skills/naming) · ★2)
- **{{skills.prefix}}-stack**: Start: a file of an active language stack changes or a stack check runs. Action: fix the owner, path, and shared contract, judge every executed logic, verify only through the stack's allowed checks, and observe the affected path from the same build when runtime behavior changed. End: the stack's checks pass with error 0 and warn 0 and no forbidden command was used as verification. ([GarnetRapture/SimpleEngineering](https://github.com/GarnetRapture/SimpleEngineering/tree/HEAD/src/template/skills/stack) · ★2)
- **{{skills.prefix}}-structure**: Start: an implementation adds, moves, or renames a file, folder, type, constant, function, or tool module. Action: fix the domain, class, owner, dependency direction, and name from the conventions before the file is written, then connect every consumer. End: one owner per concept and every caller connected. ([GarnetRapture/SimpleEngineering](https://github.com/GarnetRapture/SimpleEngineering/tree/HEAD/src/template/skills/structure) · ★2)
- **{{skills.prefix}}-subagent**: Start: a bounded research, review, or drafting task could be delegated, or a subagent answer is about to be used. Action: write the task to a scratch file, run the registered subagent through the launcher, and verify every claim of the answer against its sources before use. End: only verified claims enter the work, and the subagent id and log path are recorded. ([GarnetRapture/SimpleEngineering](https://github.com/GarnetRapture/SimpleEngineering/tree/HEAD/src/template/skills/subagent) · ★2)
- **{{skills.prefix}}-verify**: Start: project code or a tool is about to run, a result is about to be stated, or a fixed check is due. Action: judge every logic the run executes against all convention clauses in dependency order, then apply the fixed check of each change type once the instructed implementation is connected. End: every executed logic is O and every required check reports its exit and counts. ([GarnetRapture/SimpleEngineering](https://github.com/GarnetRapture/SimpleEngineering/tree/HEAD/src/template/skills/verify) · ★2)
- **{{skills.prefix}}-workflow**: Start: authorized {{project.name}} work of any kind (code in any stack, data, documents, investigation, review). - **{{task_prefix}}bug-fixer**: Tuesday and Thursday: prove one triaged bug from the code and open a small, tested fix pull request ([exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness](https://github.com/exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness/tree/HEAD/routines/bug-fixer))
- **{{task_prefix}}bug-hunter**: Weekly (Monday): review one area of the code, rotating each week, plus last week's commits, and file confirmed bugs as issues ([exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness](https://github.com/exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness/tree/HEAD/routines/bug-hunter))
- **{{task_prefix}}ci-doctor**: Every 2 hours (worker on Claude Sonnet): when the project's long CI workflow (end-to-end or integration tests) fails on an open pull request, find the cause, say on the pull request whether it caused it, re-run other failures once per commit and keep one issue per flaky test ([exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness](https://github.com/exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness/tree/HEAD/routines/ci-doctor))
- **{{task_prefix}}conflict-fixer**: Every 3 hours, on the maintainer's and the routines' open pull requests with merge conflicts, merge the default branch, resolve the conflicts, run the CI checks and push a signed merge commit to the PR branch ([exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness](https://github.com/exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness/tree/HEAD/routines/conflict-fixer))
- **{{task_prefix}}feature-planner**: Twice a week: the crew's product owner - turns ideas a maintainer marked needs-spec into ready-to-build issues (user story, acceptance criteria, how to verify, sub-issues), and once a week proposes missing features from the roadmap and milestones. Writes issues, never code. ([exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness](https://github.com/exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness/tree/HEAD/routines/feature-planner))
- **{{task_prefix}}hotfix-developer**: Every 30 min (07:17-23:47): if a priority: critical issue has a maintainer's agent-ready or ready label and no pull request yet, develop it now with the issue developer's ultracode workflow; otherwise stop in seconds ([exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness](https://github.com/exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness/tree/HEAD/routines/hotfix-developer))
- **{{task_prefix}}hotfix-review**: Hourly (07:37 to 23:37), if a critical pull request has no Claude review at its head, review it now with the pr-review routine's method (Claude Fable); otherwise stop in seconds ([exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness](https://github.com/exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness/tree/HEAD/routines/hotfix-review))
- **{{task_prefix}}issue-developer**: 5 times a day (every 4 h) - develop one agent-ready issue of {{repo}} in ultracode mode, then commit, push and open a pull request as {{bot_app}}[bot] ([exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness](https://github.com/exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness/tree/HEAD/routines/issue-developer))
- **{{task_prefix}}issue-triage**: Daily: first response, labels and analysis for new issues, puts issues without a milestone into the right existing one, then fills the organisation issue fields (Priority, Effort, Start date, Target date) on every open issue, sub-issue, epic and bug (pull requests go to the pr-review routine) ([exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness](https://github.com/exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness/tree/HEAD/routines/issue-triage))
- **{{task_prefix}}market-research**: Weekly (Wednesday): research user needs and upstream changes, open up to 2 feature proposals ([exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness](https://github.com/exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness/tree/HEAD/routines/market-research))
- **{{task_prefix}}pr-metadata**: Every 3 hours (worker on Claude Sonnet): labels, assignee, milestone and project board status on every open pull request; requests the maintainer's review, and comments and notifies once Claude's review and fixes leave the pull request ready ([exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness](https://github.com/exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness/tree/HEAD/routines/pr-metadata))
- **{{task_prefix}}pr-review**: 4 times a day, a code review and a security review of every open pull request by Claude Fable (a different model from the one that writes the routine pull requests), a counter-review of the replies in its threads, and the resolution of its own outdated threads once the finding is fixed ([exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness](https://github.com/exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness/tree/HEAD/routines/pr-review))
- **{{task_prefix}}review-fixer**: 4 times a day, on the maintainer's and the routines' open pull requests, fix what trusted reviewers asked for, push commits authored by the bot app to the PR branch and reply in every review thread ([exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness](https://github.com/exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness/tree/HEAD/routines/review-fixer))
- **{{task_prefix}}security-watch**: Every 2 h: new CVEs and advisories for the components listed in security-watch.json (GitHub advisories, OSV distro trackers, NVD, CISA KEV, pinned releases, daily web pass); Telegram alert first, then issues and an operator digest ([exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness](https://github.com/exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness/tree/HEAD/routines/security-watch))
- **{{task_prefix}}sentinel**: Every 30 min (07:00-23:30): tells the maintainers on Telegram and desktop about critical issues, advisories, do-not-merge reviews, a red default branch, unreleased critical fixes and routine failures, once each; opens an issue when the default branch stays red ([exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness](https://github.com/exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness/tree/HEAD/routines/sentinel))
- **{{task_prefix}}status-board**: Twice daily (08:30, 19:30) - collect GitHub and routine data into one snapshot and write it to the {{project}} status board artifact ([exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness](https://github.com/exceev-consulting/agent-crew-harness/tree/HEAD/routines/status-board))

- **рилс-конструктор**: Собирает у владельца проект рендера и пишет код сцен по спецификации docs/02_МЕХАНИКИ-СЦЕН.md, затем собирает конфигурацию ролика по раскадровке и делает лист кадров. Вызывается один раз при установке и дальше — когда нужна новая сцена. ([viktorshab/reels-bez-semki](https://github.com/viktorshab/reels-bez-semki/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/%D1%80%D0%B8%D0%BB%D1%81-%D0%BA%D0%BE%D0%BD%D1%81%D1%82%D1%80%D1%83%D0%BA%D1%82%D0%BE%D1%80))
- **рилс-озвучка**: Делает голос ролика (ElevenLabs или запись владельца) и получает список слов с таймингами через AssemblyAI. Вызывать после того, как текст утверждён. Тайминги слов — фундамент всего монтажа, на глаз их ставить нельзя. ([viktorshab/reels-bez-semki](https://github.com/viktorshab/reels-bez-semki/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/%D1%80%D0%B8%D0%BB%D1%81-%D0%BE%D0%B7%D0%B2%D1%83%D1%87%D0%BA%D0%B0))
- **рилс-предметы**: Генерирует предметы для кадра в едином стиле, вырезает фон и пополняет библиотеку проекта. Вызывать, когда раскадровка требует предмет, которого нет в библиотеке. Предметы — то, что держит кадр вместо плашек с текстом. ([viktorshab/reels-bez-semki](https://github.com/viktorshab/reels-bez-semki/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/%D1%80%D0%B8%D0%BB%D1%81-%D0%BF%D1%80%D0%B5%D0%B4%D0%BC%D0%B5%D1%82%D1%8B))
- **рилс-приёмщик**: Находит дефекты до рендера, рендерит ролик и проверяет результат — дыры на пустом холсте, слова не из речи, лист кадров глазами, замер статики, технические параметры файла. Вызывать перед каждым рендером и после него. Без него ролик уходит с браком, который видно зрителю. ([viktorshab/reels-bez-semki](https://github.com/viktorshab/reels-bez-semki/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/%D1%80%D0%B8%D0%BB%D1%81-%D0%BF%D1%80%D0%B8%D1%91%D0%BC%D1%89%D0%B8%D0%BA))
- **рилс-раскадровщик**: Превращает фразы речи в раскадровку — какая фраза какой сценой показывается, что в кадре и на каком слове появляется. Вызывать после озвучки, до любой вёрстки. Это главный гейт: здесь спорить дёшево, после рендера дорого. ([viktorshab/reels-bez-semki](https://github.com/viktorshab/reels-bez-semki/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/%D1%80%D0%B8%D0%BB%D1%81-%D1%80%D0%B0%D1%81%D0%BA%D0%B0%D0%B4%D1%80%D0%BE%D0%B2%D1%89%D0%B8%D0%BA))
- **рилс-сценарист**: Пишет текст графического рилса под механику сцен — ярлыки, числа вслух, пары «не X, а Y», конкретный призыв. Вызывать в начале работы над роликом, когда текста ещё нет или он написан как обычная реклама. Не монтирует и не рисует. - **‘如果想法是事实’三步探询法**: 当来访者将自动思维坚称为不可辩驳的事实时，通过结构化三问探询其信念的深层含义、现实应对资源及认知功能适配性：①该事实意味着什么（识别中间/核心信念）；②如何应对即成事实（情境模拟训练问题解决能力）；③心理教育：接纳个体差异、发展性视角、功能导向评估。 ([ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill](https://github.com/ECNU-ICALK/AutoSkill/tree/HEAD/SkillBank/DocSkill/%E5%BF%83%E7%90%86%E5%92%A8%E8%AF%A2/Family%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E8%AE%A4%E7%9F%A5%E8%A1%8C%E4%B8%BA%E7%96%97%E6%B3%95/%E5%BE%AE%E6%8A%80%E8%83%BD/%E2%80%98%E5%A6%82%E6%9E%9C%E6%83%B3%E6%B3%95%E6%98%AF%E4%BA%8B%E5%AE%9E%E2%80%99%E4%B8%89%E6%AD%A5%E6%8E%A2%E8%AF%A2%E6%B3%95) · ★620 · 4 copies)

- **チェックリストの作成**: ①作業を要素に分け、フォルダーごとに1枚のチェックリスト（HTML）を置いて進める手順。表は、作業内容と状態（完了・作業中・未着手）の2列だけにする。 ②チェックリストの行ごとに、設計書の HTML を1枚作り、行からリンクする。設計書は PlantUML の図が中心で、図の中の番号から詳しい説明をたどれる形にする。こみ入った作業は、具体的な名前の子のフォルダーに分け、親と子のチェックリストをリンクでつなぐ。md は作らず、人と AI が同じ HTML を見て直す。 ③3手を超える作業を始めるとき、「チェックリストで」「チェックリストを作って」「チェック表で」「タスクに分けて」「作業を分解して」と言われたときに使う。 ([hatohato-lab/claude-code-checklist](https://github.com/hatohato-lab/claude-code-checklist/tree/HEAD/skills/%E3%83%81%E3%82%A7%E3%83%83%E3%82%AF%E3%83%AA%E3%82%B9%E3%83%88%E3%81%AE%E4%BD%9C%E6%88%90))
